87: 
88: def sha_bytes(value: bytes) -> str:
89:     return hashlib.sha256(value).hexdigest()
90: 
91: 
92: def sha_file(path: Path) -> str:
93:     digest = hashlib.sha256()
94:     with path.open("rb") as stream:
95:         for block in iter(lambda: stream.read(65536), b""):
96:             digest.update(block)
97:     return digest.hexdigest()
98: 
99: 
100: def canonical(value: object) -> bytes:
101:     return json.dumps(value, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":"), ensure_ascii=True).encode()
102: 
103: 
104: def save_json(path: Path, value: object) -> None:
105:     path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
106:     path.write_text(json.dumps(value, indent=2, sort_keys=True) + "\n")
107: 
108: 
109: def safe_environment(home: Path) -> dict[str, str]:
110:     environment = {
111:         # Claude's installed OAuth/keychain login is part of its normal model API.
112:         # Restricted mode, empty setting sources, strict MCP config and no builtins
113:         # prevent that account home from becoming model-visible workspace context.
114:         "HOME": os.environ["HOME"],
115:         "PATH": "/Users/cburgosro/.local/bin:/Users/cburgosro/.nvm/versions/node/v24.15.0/bin:/usr/bin:/bin:/usr/sbin:/sbin",
116:         "TMPDIR": str(home / "tmp"),
117:         "LANG": "en_US.UTF-8",
118:     }
119:     for name in ("USER", "LOGNAME", "SHELL"):
120:         if name in os.environ:
121:             environment[name] = os.environ[name]
122:     return environment
123: 
124: 
125: def run_bounded(
126:     argv: list[str], cwd: Path, environment: dict[str, str], timeout: int, artifact: Path
127: ) -> tuple[int, bytes, bytes, float]:
128:     started = time.monotonic()
129:     child = subprocess.Popen(
130:         argv,
131:         cwd=cwd,
132:         env=environment,
133:         stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL,
134:         stdout=subprocess.PIPE,
135:         stderr=subprocess.PIPE,
136:         start_new_session=True,
137:     )
138:     selector = selectors.DefaultSelector()
139:     buffers = {"stdout": bytearray(), "stderr": bytearray()}
140:     total = 0
141:     failure = None
142:     failed_at = None
143:     deadline = started + timeout
144:     for name, pipe in (("stdout", child.stdout), ("stderr", child.stderr)):
145:         os.set_blocking(pipe.fileno(), False)
146:         selector.register(pipe, selectors.EVENT_READ, name)
147:     while child.poll() is None or selector.get_map():
148:         now = time.monotonic()
149:         if failure is None and now >= deadline:
150:             failure = "command_timeout"
151:             failed_at = now
152:             try:
153:                 os.killpg(child.pid, signal.SIGKILL)
154:             except ProcessLookupError:
155:                 pass
156:         for key, _ in selector.select(0.05):
157:             try:
158:                 block = os.read(key.fileobj.fileno(), 65536)
159:             except BlockingIOError:
160:                 continue
161:             if not block:
162:                 selector.unregister(key.fileobj)
163:                 key.fileobj.close()
164:                 continue
165:             total += len(block)
166:             remaining = max(0, MAX_OUTPUT - sum(len(value) for value in buffers.values()))
167:             buffers[key.data].extend(block[:remaining])
168:             if failure is None and total > MAX_OUTPUT:
169:                 failure = "command_output_limit"
170:                 failed_at = time.monotonic()
171:                 try:
172:                     os.killpg(child.pid, signal.SIGKILL)
173:                 except ProcessLookupError:
174:                     pass
175:         if failure is not None and child.poll() is not None and not selector.get_map():
176:             break
177:         if failed_at is not None and time.monotonic() - failed_at >= 10:
178:             for registered in list(selector.get_map().values()):
179:                 selector.unregister(registered.fileobj)
180:                 registered.fileobj.close()
181:             break
182:     selector.close()
183:     child.wait()
184:     stdout, stderr = bytes(buffers["stdout"]), bytes(buffers["stderr"])
185:     duration = time.monotonic() - started
186:     artifact.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
187:     artifact.with_suffix(".stdout").write_bytes(stdout)
188:     artifact.with_suffix(".stderr").write_bytes(stderr)
189:     save_json(
190:         artifact,
191:         {
192:             "argv": argv,
193:             "exit_code": child.returncode,
194:             "duration_seconds": round(duration, 3),
195:             "stdout_bytes": len(stdout),
196:             "stdout_sha256": sha_bytes(stdout),
197:             "stderr_bytes": len(stderr),
198:             "stderr_sha256": sha_bytes(stderr),
199:             "status": "failed" if failure else "complete",
200:             "failure": failure,
201:             "observed_output_bytes": total,
202:         },
203:     )
204:     if failure is not None:
205:         raise RuntimeError(failure)
206:     return child.returncode, stdout, stderr, duration
207: 
208: 
209: def copy_vendor(destination: Path) -> None:
210:     source = ROOT / "fixtures/cargo-vendor-data/vendor"
211:     shutil.copytree(source, destination, symlinks=False)
212:     for item in destination.rglob("*"):
213:         if item.is_symlink() or not (item.is_dir() or item.is_file()):
214:             raise RuntimeError("vendor_special_entry")
215: 
216: 
217: def create_fixture(run: Path) -> dict[str, Path]:
218:     project = run / "project"
219:     state = run / "state"
220:     home = run / "home"
221:     for directory in (project / "src", state, home / "tmp"):
222:         directory.mkdir(parents=True, mode=0o700)
223:     (project / "Cargo.toml").write_bytes(INITIAL_MANIFEST)
224:     (project / "Cargo.lock").write_bytes(LOCK)
225:     (project / "src/lib.rs").write_bytes(INITIAL_SOURCE)
226:     vendor = run / "vendor"
227:     copy_vendor(vendor)
228:     return {"project": project, "state": state, "home": home, "vendor": vendor}
229: 
230: 
231: def snapshot(root: Path) -> dict[str, str]:
232:     result = {}
233:     for path in sorted(root.rglob("*")):
234:         if path.is_symlink():
235:             raise RuntimeError("fixture_symlink")
236:         if path.is_file():
237:             result[str(path.relative_to(root))] = sha_file(path)
238:     return result
239: 
240: 
241: def server_args(paths: dict[str, Path], socket: str, grants: bool) -> list[str]:
242:     arguments = [
243:         "serve", "--stdio", "--root", str(paths["project"]),
244:         "--docker", str(DOCKER), "--docker-socket", socket,
245:         "--state-root", str(paths["state"]), "--rust-image", IMAGE,
246:     ]
247:     if grants:
248:         for flag in (
249:             "--allow-manifest-write", "--allow-fmt-write", "--allow-fix-write",
250:             "--allow-dependency-add", "--allow-dependency-remove",
251:         ):
252:             arguments += [flag, str(paths["project"])]
253:         arguments += [
254:             "--cargo-vendor-dir", str(paths["vendor"]),
255:             "--cargo-vendor-tree-sha256", VENDOR_FP,
256:         ]
257:     return arguments
258: 
259: 
260: def inspector_call(
261:     run: Path,
262:     environment: dict[str, str],
263:     label: str,
264:     args: list[str],
265:     method: str,
266:     tool: str | None = None,
267:     tool_args: dict | None = None,
268:     deadline: float | None = None,
269:     expected_tool_error: bool = False,
270: ) -> dict:
271:     argv = [str(NODE), str(INSPECTOR), str(SERVER), *args, "--", "--method", method,
272:             "--format", "json", "--stored-auth-only"]
273:     if method == "tools/list":
274:         argv.append("--strict")
275:     if tool is not None:
276:         argv += ["--tool-name", tool, "--tool-args-json", json.dumps(tool_args, separators=(",", ":"))]
277:     remaining = 180 if deadline is None else min(180, int(deadline - time.monotonic()))
278:     if remaining < 1:
279:         raise RuntimeError("qualification_wall_timeout")
280:     code, stdout, _, _ = run_bounded(argv, run, environment, remaining, ARTIFACTS / f"inspector-{label}.json")
281:     expected_code = 5 if expected_tool_error else 0
282:     if code != expected_code:
283:         raise RuntimeError(f"inspector_{label}_exit_{code}")
284:     response = json.loads(stdout)
285:     if expected_tool_error:
286:         result = response.get("result", response)
287:         if not isinstance(result, dict) or result.get("isError") is not True:
288:             raise RuntimeError(f"inspector_{label}_missing_tool_error")
289:     return response
290: 
291: 
292: def structured(result: dict) -> dict:
293:     value = result.get("result", result)
294:     content = value.get("structuredContent") if isinstance(value, dict) else None
295:     if not isinstance(content, dict):
296:         raise RuntimeError("missing_structured_content")
297:     return content
298: 
299: 
300: def inspect_stock_client(run: Path, paths: dict[str, Path], socket: str, deadline: float) -> dict:
301:     environment = safe_environment(paths["home"])
302:     denied_args = server_args(paths, socket, False)
303:     discovery = inspector_call(run, environment, "discovery", denied_args, "tools/list", deadline=deadline)
304:     tools = discovery.get("result", discovery).get("tools")
305:     if not isinstance(tools, list) or [tool.get("name") for tool in tools] != list(EXPECTED_TOOLS):
306:         raise RuntimeError("inspector_tool_inventory")
307:     by_name = {tool["name"]: tool for tool in tools}
308:     snapshot_dir = ROOT / "crates/mcp-server/tests/snapshots"
309:     unchanged = {}
310:     for name, filename in M1_SNAPSHOTS.items():
311:         expected = json.loads((snapshot_dir / filename).read_text())
312:         if by_name[name] != expected:
313:             raise RuntimeError(f"m1_snapshot_changed_{name}")
314:         unchanged[name] = sha_bytes(canonical(expected))
315:     opened = inspector_call(
316:         run, environment, "open", denied_args, "tools/call", "rust.project.open",
317:         {"path": str(paths["project"])}, deadline=deadline,
318:     )
319:     if structured(opened).get("status") != "passed":
320:         raise RuntimeError("inspector_open_failed")
321:     fake_ref = "prj_" + "0" * 32
322:     fake_fp = "sha256:" + "0" * 64
323:     calls = {
324:         "rust.manifest.patch": {"project_ref": fake_ref, "action": {"mode": "preview", "expected_project_fingerprint": fake_fp, "edit": {"operation": "lint_set", "scope": "package", "tool": "rust", "name": "unsafe_code", "level": "forbid", "priority": 0}}},
325:         "rust.fmt.apply": {"project_ref": fake_ref, "action": {"mode": "preview", "expected_project_fingerprint": fake_fp}},
326:         "rust.fix.apply": {"project_ref": fake_ref, "action": {"mode": "preview", "expected_project_fingerprint": fake_fp}},
327:         "rust.dependency.add": {"project_ref": fake_ref, "action": {"mode": "preview", "expected_project_fingerprint": fake_fp, "name": "quote", "requirement": "=1.0.47"}},
328:         "rust.dependency.remove": {"project_ref": fake_ref, "action": {"mode": "preview", "expected_project_fingerprint": fake_fp, "name": "quote"}},
329:     }
330:     denied = {}
331:     for index, (name, arguments) in enumerate(calls.items(), 1):
332:         response = inspector_call(
333:             run, environment, f"deny-{index}", denied_args, "tools/call", name,
334:             arguments, deadline, expected_tool_error=True,
335:         )
336:         output = structured(response)
337:         if output.get("status") != "blocked" or output.get("error_code") != "permission_denied":
338:             raise RuntimeError(f"inspector_default_deny_{name}")
339:         denied[name] = output["error_code"]
340:     return {
341:         "version": json.loads(INSPECTOR_PACKAGE.read_text())["version"],
342:         "cli_sha256": sha_file(INSPECTOR),
343:         "node_sha256": sha_file(NODE),
344:         "tool_count": len(tools),
345:         "tool_names": list(EXPECTED_TOOLS),
346:         "canonical_inventory_sha256": sha_bytes(canonical(tools)),
347:         "m1_snapshots": unchanged,
348:         "positive_open": True,
349:         "default_denials": denied,
350:         "limitation": "Inspector CLI is one-shot; project references cannot be carried into a later invocation.",
351:     }
352: 
353: 
354: def claude_config(paths: dict[str, Path], socket: str) -> dict:
355:     return {
356:         "mcpServers": {
357:             "rust_engineering": {
358:                 "command": str(SERVER),
359:                 "args": server_args(paths, socket, True),
360:                 "env": {},
361:             }
362:         }
363:     }
364: 
365: 
366: def claude_prompt(project: Path) -> str:
367:     return f"""Qualify the Rust Engineering MCP using only its MCP tools. The authorized scratch project is {project}.
368: Discover the available MCP tools and use rust.project.open. Then complete these five mutations sequentially, reopening the project after every commit so you always use a current project reference:
369: 1. Add dependency key `quoted` as package `quote`, exact requirement `=1.0.47`, normal dependency, feature `proc-macro`, optional false, default features false. Preview first, inspect the complete returned diff and validation, then commit.
370: 2. Remove exactly dependency key `quoted`. Preview and review the complete diff and validation, commit, then reopen. The lock must return to the std-only state before later operations.
371: 3. Apply a package Rust lint setting `unsafe_code = forbid` with priority omitted (no explicit priority) using rust.manifest.patch. Preview and review, commit, reopen.
372: 4. Apply rust.fmt.apply. Preview and review, commit, reopen.
373: 5. Apply rust.fix.apply to remove the existing unused mut warning. Preview and review, commit, reopen, then retrieve that operation's receipt.
374: Reference rule for every operation: commit invalidates its input project_ref. Each reopen returns a NEW data.project_ref. Discard the old reference and copy the exact newly returned value into every following call, including the final Fix receipt. Immediately before the final receipt, state which newly returned reference you will use. Never use the reference from Fix preview/commit for its receipt.
375: Do not call any non-MCP tool. Do not skip a preview or commit. Stop on any blocked, failed, cancelled or unavailable result. Finish with a concise account of the operations and whether every reviewed candidate matched the requested scope."""
376: 
377: 
378: def walk_json(value: object):
379:     yield value
380:     if isinstance(value, dict):
381:         for child in value.values():
382:             yield from walk_json(child)
383:     elif isinstance(value, list):
384:         for child in value:
385:             yield from walk_json(child)
386: 
387: 
388: def normalize_tool(name: str) -> str | None:
389:     if not name.startswith("mcp__rust_engineering__"):
390:         return None
391:     suffix = name.removeprefix("mcp__rust_engineering__")
392:     for expected in ("rust.project.open", *M2_TOOLS):
393:         if suffix in (expected, expected.replace(".", "_")):
394:             return expected
395:     return f"unexpected:{suffix}"
396: 
397: 
398: def validate_claude_stream(raw: bytes) -> dict:
399:     calls = []
400:     by_id = {}
401:     results = {}
402:     models = set()
403:     events = []
404:     for line in raw.splitlines():
405:         if not line.strip():
406:             continue
407:         event = json.loads(line)
408:         events.append(event)
409:         for value in walk_json(event):
410:             if not isinstance(value, dict):
411:                 continue
412:             model = value.get("model")
413:             if isinstance(model, str) and "claude" in model:
414:                 models.add(model)
415:             if value.get("type") == "tool_use" and isinstance(value.get("name"), str):
416:                 normalized = normalize_tool(value["name"])
417:                 if normalized is None or normalized.startswith("unexpected:"):
418:                     raise RuntimeError(f"claude_forbidden_tool_{value['name']}")
419:                 identifier = value.get("id")
420:                 if not isinstance(identifier, str) or identifier in by_id:
421:                     raise RuntimeError("claude_duplicate_or_missing_tool_use_id")
422:                 call = {"id": identifier, "tool": normalized, "input": value.get("input")}
423:                 calls.append(call)
424:                 by_id[identifier] = call
425:             if value.get("type") == "tool_result":
426:                 identifier = value.get("tool_use_id")
427:                 if not isinstance(identifier, str) or identifier in results:
428:                     raise RuntimeError("claude_duplicate_or_missing_tool_result_id")
429:                 results[identifier] = value
430:     if not events or events[-1].get("type") != "result" or events[-1].get("subtype") != "success" or events[-1].get("is_error") is True:
431:         raise RuntimeError("claude_missing_success_final_event")
432:     names = [call["tool"] for call in calls]
433:     if not models or any(not model.startswith(MODEL) for model in models):
434:         raise RuntimeError("claude_model_identity_or_fallback")
435:     for tool in M2_TOOLS:
436:         modes = [call.get("input", {}).get("action", {}).get("mode") for call in calls if call["tool"] == tool]
437:         if "preview" not in modes or "commit" not in modes:
438:             raise RuntimeError(f"claude_missing_preview_commit_{tool}")
439:     fix_modes = [call.get("input", {}).get("action", {}).get("mode") for call in calls if call["tool"] == "rust.fix.apply"]
440:     if "receipt" not in fix_modes or names.count("rust.project.open") < 6:
441:         raise RuntimeError("claude_missing_reopen_or_receipt")
442:     if set(results) != set(by_id):
443:         raise RuntimeError("claude_unmatched_tool_results")
444:     observations = []
445:     for identifier, call in by_id.items():
446:         result = results[identifier]
447:         if result.get("is_error") is True:
448:             raise RuntimeError(f"claude_failed_tool_result_{call['tool']}")
449:         candidates = []
450:         for value in walk_json(result.get("content")):
451:             if isinstance(value, dict) and isinstance(value.get("structuredContent"), dict):
452:                 candidates.append(value["structuredContent"])
453:             elif isinstance(value, str):
454:                 try:
455:                     parsed = json.loads(value)
456:                 except json.JSONDecodeError:
457:                     continue
458:                 for parsed_value in walk_json(parsed):
459:                     if isinstance(parsed_value, dict) and "status" in parsed_value:
460:                         candidates.append(parsed_value)
461:         outputs = [value for value in candidates if value.get("status") in {"passed", "failed", "blocked", "cancelled", "unavailable"}]
462:         if len(outputs) != 1 or outputs[0].get("status") != "passed":
463:             raise RuntimeError(f"claude_nonpassing_or_ambiguous_tool_result_{call['tool']}")
464:         action = call.get("input", {}).get("action", {})
465:         mode = action.get("mode") if isinstance(action, dict) else None
466:         if mode == "commit" and outputs[0].get("data", {}).get("state") != "committed":
467:             raise RuntimeError(f"claude_commit_not_committed_{call['tool']}")
468:         if mode == "receipt" and outputs[0].get("data", {}).get("state") != "committed":
469:             raise RuntimeError(f"claude_receipt_not_committed_{call['tool']}")
470:         observations.append({"tool_use_id": identifier, "tool": call["tool"], "mode": mode, "status": "passed"})
471:     return {"calls": calls, "results": observations, "tool_call_count": len(calls), "tool_result_count": len(results), "observed_models": sorted(models), "final_event_subtype": "success"}
472: 
473: 
474: def run_claude(run: Path, paths: dict[str, Path], socket: str, deadline: float) -> dict:
475:     config = run / "claude-mcp.json"
476:     save_json(config, claude_config(paths, socket))
477:     allowed = [
478:         "mcp__rust_engineering__rust.project.open",
479:         *[f"mcp__rust_engineering__{name}" for name in M2_TOOLS],
480:         "mcp__rust_engineering__rust_project_open",
481:         *[f"mcp__rust_engineering__{name.replace('.', '_')}" for name in M2_TOOLS],
482:     ]
483:     argv = [
484:         str(CLAUDE), "--print", "--output-format", "stream-json", "--verbose",
485:         "--model", MODEL, "--effort", EFFORT, "--no-session-persistence",
486:         "--restricted", "--setting-sources", "", "--strict-mcp-config",
487:         "--mcp-config", str(config), "--disable-slash-commands", "--no-chrome",
488:         "--tools", "", "--allowedTools", ",".join(allowed),
489:         "--permission-mode", "dontAsk", "--permission-prompts", "none",
490:         "--system-prompt", "You are a bounded third-party MCP client qualifier. Use only the explicitly configured Rust Engineering MCP server and follow the requested preview-review-commit workflow.",
491:         claude_prompt(paths["project"]),
492:     ]
493:     remaining = int(deadline - time.monotonic())
494:     if remaining < 1:
495:         raise RuntimeError("qualification_wall_timeout")
496:     code, stdout, stderr, duration = run_bounded(
497:         argv, run, safe_environment(paths["home"]), remaining,
498:         ARTIFACTS / "claude-model-session.json",
499:     )
500:     if code != 0:
501:         raise RuntimeError(f"claude_exit_{code}:stderr_sha256={sha_bytes(stderr)}")
502:     stream = validate_claude_stream(stdout)
503:     actual_tree = snapshot(paths["project"])
504:     expected_bytes = {"Cargo.toml": FINAL_MANIFEST, "Cargo.lock": LOCK, "src/lib.rs": FINAL_SOURCE}
505:     expected_tree = {name: sha_bytes(value) for name, value in expected_bytes.items()}
506:     if actual_tree != expected_tree:
507:         raise RuntimeError("claude_final_source_mismatch")
508:     return {
509:         "version": subprocess.run([str(CLAUDE), "--version"], capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=10, check=True).stdout.strip(),
510:         "executable_sha256": sha_file(CLAUDE),
511:         "model_requested": MODEL,
512:         "effort_requested": EFFORT,
513:         "fallback_configured": False,
514:         "builtins": [],
515:         "allowed_mcp_tools": ["rust.project.open", *M2_TOOLS],
516:         "duration_seconds": round(duration, 3),
517:         "stderr_sha256": sha_bytes(stderr),
518:         "stream": stream,
519:         "final_tree_sha256": actual_tree,
520:     }
521: 
522: 
523: def docker_inventory(run: Path, socket: str) -> dict[str, list[str]]:
524:     config = run / "docker-config"
525:     config.mkdir(exist_ok=True, mode=0o700)
526:     (config / "config.json").write_text("{}\n")
527:     inventory = {}
528:     for kind in ("container", "volume"):
529:         argv = [str(DOCKER), "--config", str(config), "--host", f"unix://{socket}", kind, "ls"]
530:         if kind == "container":
531:             argv += ["--all", "--no-trunc"]
532:         argv += ["--filter", "label=org.rust-mcp.execution=true", "--format", "{{.Names}}" if kind == "container" else "{{.Name}}"]
533:         result = subprocess.run(argv, env={}, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=15, check=True)
534:         inventory[kind + "s"] = [line for line in result.stdout.splitlines() if line]
535:     return inventory
536: 
537: 
538: def prerequisites() -> dict:
539:     required = (NODE, INSPECTOR, INSPECTOR_PACKAGE, CLAUDE, SERVER, DOCKER)
540:     missing = [str(path) for path in required if not path.is_file()]
541:     if missing:
542:         raise RuntimeError("missing_prerequisites:" + ",".join(missing))
543:     version = json.loads(INSPECTOR_PACKAGE.read_text())["version"]
544:     if version != "2.5.0":
545:         raise RuntimeError(f"inspector_version_{version}")
546:     claude_version = subprocess.run([str(CLAUDE), "--version"], capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=10, check=True).stdout.strip()
547:     if not claude_version.startswith("2.1.260"):
548:         raise RuntimeError(f"claude_version_{claude_version}")
549:     (WORK / "tmp").mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True, mode=0o700)
550:     auth = subprocess.run(
551:         [str(CLAUDE), "auth", "status"],
552:         env=safe_environment(WORK),
553:         capture_output=True,
554:         timeout=10,
555:     )
556:     try:
557:         auth_status = json.loads(auth.stdout)
558:     except (json.JSONDecodeError, UnicodeDecodeError) as error:
559:         raise RuntimeError("claude_auth_status_invalid") from error
560:     if auth.returncode != 0 or auth_status.get("loggedIn") is not True:
561:         raise RuntimeError("claude_normal_api_auth_unavailable")
562:     return {
563:         "inspector_version": version,
564:         "inspector_cli_sha256": sha_file(INSPECTOR),
565:         "node_sha256": sha_file(NODE),
566:         "claude_version": claude_version,
567:         "claude_sha256": sha_file(CLAUDE),
568:         "claude_auth_method": auth_status.get("authMethod"),
569:         "server_sha256": sha_file(SERVER),
570:     }
571: 
572: 
573: def main() -> int:
574:     parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
575:     parser.add_argument("--run", action="store_true", help="execute the serialized Docker/model gate")
576:     parser.add_argument("--docker-socket", default=os.environ.get("RUST_MCP_TEST_SOCKET"))
577:     options = parser.parse_args()
578:     identities = prerequisites()
579:     prepared = {
580:         "schema": "rust-mcp-m2-third-party-clients-v2",
581:         "status": "prepared" if not options.run else "running",
582:         "scope": "Inspector one-shot discovery/default-deny plus one stock Claude model-directed five-tool mutation workflow",
583:         "limits": {"wall_seconds": WALL_SECONDS, "combined_output_bytes": MAX_OUTPUT},
584:         "identities": identities,
585:         "inspector_exit_5_disposition": {
586:             "installed_source": "target/m1-17-inspector/node_modules/@modelcontextprotocol/inspector/clients/cli/build/index.js",
587:             "installed_source_sha256": identities["inspector_cli_sha256"],
588:             "exit_code_definition_lines": "13255-13262",
589:             "is_error_mapping_lines": "13741-13746",
590:             "semantics": "Inspector 2.5.0 emits the JSON result, then exits 5 when result.isError is true; only the five expected default-deny calls accept that exit and still require isError:true plus blocked/permission_denied.",
591:         },
592:         "expected_tools": list(EXPECTED_TOOLS),
593:         "expected_model_calls": {
594:             "rust.project.open": "at least six",
595:             **{tool: ["preview", "commit"] for tool in M2_TOOLS},
596:             "rust.fix.apply_receipt": True,
597:         },
598:         "security": {
599:             "claude_restricted": True,
600:             "builtin_tools": [],
601:             "strict_mcp_config": True,
602:             "permission_bypass": False,
603:             "fallback_model": False,
604:             "network_claim": "No client network-isolation claim; the normal model API is required. Guest execution remains Docker network=none.",
605:         },
606:     }
607:     save_json(RECEIPT, prepared)
608:     if not options.run:
609:         print(RECEIPT)
610:         return 0
611:     if not options.docker_socket or not options.docker_socket.startswith("/"):
612:         raise RuntimeError("explicit_absolute_docker_socket_required")
613:     run = WORK / f"run-{time.time_ns()}"
614:     run.mkdir(parents=True, mode=0o700)
615:     paths = create_fixture(run)
616:     prepared["run_directory"] = str(run.relative_to(ROOT))
617:     prepared["fixture_before"] = snapshot(paths["project"])
618:     prepared["vendor_fingerprint"] = VENDOR_FP
619:     prepared["docker_before"] = docker_inventory(run, options.docker_socket)
620:     if any(prepared["docker_before"].values()):
621:         raise RuntimeError("docker_not_exclusive")
622:     try:
623:         deadline = time.monotonic() + WALL_SECONDS
624:         prepared["inspector"] = inspect_stock_client(run, paths, options.docker_socket, deadline)
625:         prepared["claude"] = run_claude(run, paths, options.docker_socket, deadline)
626:         prepared["fixture_after"] = snapshot(paths["project"])
627:         prepared["docker_after"] = docker_inventory(run, options.docker_socket)
628:         if any(prepared["docker_after"].values()):
629:             raise RuntimeError("docker_cleanup_incomplete")
630:         prepared["status"] = "passed"
631:         return 0
632:     except BaseException as error:
633:         prepared["status"] = "failed"
634:         prepared["error"] = f"{type(error).__name__}:{error}"
635:         try:
636:             prepared["docker_after_failure"] = docker_inventory(run, options.docker_socket)
637:         except BaseException as inventory_error:
638:             prepared["docker_inventory_error"] = str(inventory_error)
639:         raise
640:     finally:
641:         save_json(RECEIPT, prepared)
642:         print(RECEIPT, flush=True)
643: 
644:

3:   "description": "Preview, commit or inspect a journaled semantic root Cargo.toml edit. Host --allow-manifest-write is required. Preview validates the exact candidate in the approved offline Cargo runtime and returns its diff/digest. New effects require an unexpired approved plan and an idempotency key. Exact ID/digest/key can replay an existing journal under current authority. Commit invalidates its input project_ref: call rust.project.open and use its newly returned data.project_ref for ALL later calls, including receipt/recovery; never reuse the precommit reference. Local coordinated writes do not exclude external editors or provide multi-file atomicity.
